- **mermaid-linter**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "validate Mermaid diagrams", "lint diagrams", "check diagram syntax", "mermaid health score", or needs to validate Mermaid diagrams in markdown files for syntax errors, configuration, and rendering quality.
